# Accepted differences between Mermaider and mermaid.js, as MjsFactsTests reports them. A "# reason" line explains the
# entries below it, up to the next blank line. "-" is a fact only mermaid.js has, "+" one only Mermaider has. A new
# difference fails the test, and so does a listed one that no longer occurs. Write a current list with
# MJS_FACTS_REPORT=<dir> and the MjsFactsTests/Write_difference_report test.

# By design: Mermaider reads a literal \n in a label as a line break. mermaid.js 12 draws the two characters.
rfc-path-flow + node | perRepo | Per-repo docset.yml + toc.yml | rect
rfc-path-flow - node | perRepo | Per-repo\ndocset.yml + toc.yml | rect
rfc-migration-lifecycle + edge | VirtualRemap | PhysicalRestructure | none | arrow | solid | run apply-nav-restructure (only if content moves)
rfc-migration-lifecycle - edge | VirtualRemap | PhysicalRestructure | none | arrow | solid | run apply-nav-restructure\n(only if content moves)

# By design: Mermaider reads `direction TD` in an ER diagram as a direction. mermaid.js 12.3.0 takes only TB, BT, LR and
# RL there, and draws `direction` and `TD` as two entities.
er-direction - entity | TD | TD
er-direction - entity | direction | direction
er-direction - label | TD
er-direction - label | direction
spec-er-direction-top-down - entity | TD | TD
spec-er-direction-top-down - entity | direction | direction
spec-er-direction-top-down - label | TD
spec-er-direction-top-down - label | direction

# By design: Mermaider reads quoted tree view names ("my project"/). mermaid.js 12.3.0 draws each such name empty and
# every node as a file.
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / README (important).md | file
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / config files / app config.json | file
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / config files / env vars.env | file
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / config files | dir
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / source files / helper utils.ts | file
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / source files / main module.ts | file
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project / source files | dir
treeview-quoted-labels + node | my project | dir
treeview-quoted-labels - node |  | dir
treeview-quoted-labels - node |  | dir
treeview-quoted-labels - node |  | dir
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / README (important).md | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / app config.json | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / config files | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / env vars.env | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / helper utils.ts | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / main module.ts | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | / source files | file
treeview-quoted-labels - node | my project | file

# By design: Mermaider accepts gallery sources that mermaid.js 12.3.0 refuses. mermaid.js wants an entity name where
# `A ||--|| B` has `||` ("got 'ONLY_ONE'"), requires `: label` on every ER relationship, rejects a requirement id with a
# hyphen (`id: R-0`, "Expecting 'NEWLINE', got 'LINE'"), and has no block diagram `title`.
er-cardinalities + accept | yes
er-cardinalities - accept | no
spec-er-all-cardinality-combinations + accept | yes
spec-er-all-cardinality-combinations - accept | no
er-optional-label + accept | yes
er-optional-label - accept | no
spec-er-optional-relationship-label + accept | yes
spec-er-optional-relationship-label - accept | no
requirement-kinds + accept | yes
requirement-kinds - accept | no
requirement-long-text + accept | yes
requirement-long-text - accept | no
requirement-relations + accept | yes
requirement-relations - accept | no
requirement-sysml + accept | yes
requirement-sysml - accept | no
requirement-two-systems + accept | yes
requirement-two-systems - accept | no
block-title-rounded + accept | yes
block-title-rounded - accept | no
